      /* ── Fix: win back the base styles HubSpot's theme sets ──
         The theme stylesheet loads before this block and targets bare
         elements (html, body, p, h2, button…), so it outranks the `*`
         reset at the top. These element-level rules sit later in the
         cascade and take it back. Nothing here outranks a class, so
         the design above is untouched. */
      html {
        font-size: 16px;
      }
      body {
        font-size: 16px;
        line-height: 1.5;
        word-break: normal;
      }
      p,
      h1,
      h2,
      h3,
      h4,
      h5,
      h6,
      ul,
      ol,
      blockquote {
        margin: 0;
        font-family: inherit;
        color: inherit;
      }
      a {
        font-family: inherit;
      }
      button {
        font-family: inherit;
        text-transform: none;
      }

      /* Re-assert button states the theme's button:hover/:disabled grabs */
      .btn-primary:hover,
      .btn-primary:focus,
      .btn-primary:disabled {
        background: var(--cl-blue);
        border-color: var(--cl-blue);
        color: var(--cl-white);
      }
      .btn-secondary:hover,
      .btn-secondary:focus {
        background: var(--cl-white);
        color: var(--cl-black);
        border-color: var(--cl-black-40);
      }

        var preview = document.getElementById("preview");
        var fileInput = document.getElementById("file");
        var nameInput = document.getElementById("fullname");
        var roleInput = document.getElementById("role");
        var companyInput = document.getElementById("company");
        var zoomInput = document.getElementById("zoom");
        var downloadBtn = document.getElementById("download");
        var errorMsg = document.getElementById("error-msg");
        var shareBox = document.getElementById("share-box");
        var thumb = document.getElementById("photo-thumb");
        var filename = document.getElementById("photo-filename");

        var template = new Image();
        var photo = null;
        var photoUrl = null;
        var offset = { x: 0, y: 0 };

        template.onload = function () {
          if (document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) {
            document.fonts.ready.then(render);
          } else {
            render();
          }
        };
        template.src = TEMPLATE_SRC;

        /* ---------------- text ---------------- */

        // Step the size down until the string fits the available width.
        function fit(text, base, weight) {
          var s = base;
          do {
            ctx.font = weight + " " + s + "px " + CONFIG.font;
            s -= 1;
          } while (ctx.measureText(text).width > CONFIG.maxWidth && s > 14);
          return s + 1;
        }

        // Each line sits at its own fixed baseline, so leaving one blank
        // never shifts the others.
        function line(text, cfg) {
          if (!text) return;
          ctx.font =
            cfg.weight + " " + fit(text, cfg.size, cfg.weight) + "px " + CONFIG.font;
          ctx.textAlign = "right";
          ctx.textBaseline = "alphabetic";
          ctx.shadowColor = CONFIG.shadow;
          ctx.shadowBlur = 14;
          ctx.shadowOffsetY = 1;
          ctx.fillStyle = cfg.color;
          ctx.fillText(text, CONFIG.textX, cfg.y);
          ctx.shadowColor = "transparent";
          ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
          ctx.shadowOffsetY = 0;
        }

        /* ---------------- photo ---------------- */

        function framePath() {
          var p = CONFIG.photo;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(p.cx, p.cy, p.size / 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.closePath();
        }

        // Keep the photo covering the circle so dragging can't expose a gap.
        function clampOffsets() {
          var p = CONFIG.photo;
          var base = Math.max(
            p.size / photo.naturalWidth,
            p.size / photo.naturalHeight
          );
          var w = photo.naturalWidth * base * parseFloat(zoomInput.value);
          var h = photo.naturalHeight * base * parseFloat(zoomInput.value);
          var mx = Math.max(0, (w - p.size) / 2);
          var my = Math.max(0, (h - p.size) / 2);
          offset.x = Math.max(-mx, Math.min(mx, offset.x));
          offset.y = Math.max(-my, Math.min(my, offset.y));
        }

        function drawPhoto() {
          var p = CONFIG.photo;

          // The frame is drawn even with no photo, so the card reads as
          // finished and people can see where their face will land.
          ctx.save();
          ctx.shadowColor = "rgba(0,0,0,.5)";
          ctx.shadowBlur = 26;
          ctx.shadowOffsetY = 6;
          framePath();
          ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(6,12,28,.85)";
          ctx.fill();
          ctx.restore();

          ctx.save();
          framePath();
          ctx.clip();
          if (photo) {
            clampOffsets();
            var base = Math.max(
              p.size / photo.naturalWidth,
              p.size / photo.naturalHeight
            );
            var scale = base * parseFloat(zoomInput.value);
            var w = photo.naturalWidth * scale;
            var h = photo.naturalHeight * scale;
            ctx.drawImage(
              photo,
              p.cx - w / 2 + offset.x,
              p.cy - h / 2 + offset.y,
              w,
              h
            );
          } else {
            ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,.62)";
            ctx.textAlign = "center";
            ctx.font = "500 24px " + CONFIG.font;
            ctx.fillText("Your photo", p.cx, p.cy + 8);
          }
          ctx.restore();

          if (p.ringWidth > 0) {
            framePath();
            ctx.lineWidth = p.ringWidth;
            ctx.strokeStyle = p.ring;
            ctx.stroke();
          }
        }

        /* ---------------- render ---------------- */

        function render() {
          ctx.clearRect(0, 0, 1200, 1200);
          if (template.complete) ctx.drawImage(template, 0, 0, 1200, 1200);
          drawPhoto();
          line(nameInput.value.trim(), CONFIG.name);
          line(roleInput.value.trim(), CONFIG.title);
          line(companyInput.value.trim(), CONFIG.company);
        }

        /* ---------------- inputs ---------------- */

        function loadFile(file) {
          if (!file) return;
          if (!/^image\//.test(file.type)) {
            errorMsg.style.display = "block";
            return;
          }
          errorMsg.style.display = "none";

          var url = URL.createObjectURL(file);
          var img = new Image();
          img.onload = function () {
            if (photoUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(photoUrl);
            photo = img;
            photoUrl = url;
            offset = { x: 0, y: 0 };
            zoomInput.value = 1;
            zoomInput.disabled = false;

            preview.classList.add("has-photo");
            downloadBtn.disabled = false;
            thumb.style.backgroundImage = "url(" + url + ")";
            filename.textContent = file.name;
            render();
          };
          img.onerror = function () {
            URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
            errorMsg.style.display = "block";
          };
          img.src = url;
        }

        fileInput.addEventListener("change", function (e) {
          loadFile(e.target.files[0]);
        });
        document.getElementById("pick").addEventListener("click", function () {
          fileInput.click();
        });
        document
          .getElementById("pick-inline")
          .addEventListener("click", function () {
            fileInput.click();
          });

        [nameInput, roleInput, companyInput, zoomInput].forEach(function (el) {
          el.addEventListener("input", render);
        });

        document.getElementById("rings").addEventListener("click", function (e) {
          var btn = e.target.closest(".swatch");
          if (!btn) return;
          var value = btn.getAttribute("data-ring");
          CONFIG.photo.ring = value === "none" ? "#000000" : value;
          CONFIG.photo.ringWidth = value === "none" ? 0 : 8;
          Array.prototype.forEach.call(this.children, function (c) {
            c.setAttribute("aria-pressed", c === btn ? "true" : "false");
          });
          render();
        });

        document.getElementById("reset").addEventListener("click", function () {
          if (photoUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(photoUrl);
          photo = null;
          photoUrl = null;
          fileInput.value = "";
          nameInput.value = "";
          roleInput.value = "";
          companyInput.value = "";
          zoomInput.value = 1;
          zoomInput.disabled = true;
          thumb.style.backgroundImage = "";
          filename.textContent = "No photo selected";
          preview.classList.remove("has-photo");
          downloadBtn.disabled = true;
          shareBox.style.display = "none";
          errorMsg.style.display = "none";
          render();
        });

        // Drag to reposition
        var dragging = false;
        var last = null;
        canvas.addEventListener("pointerdown", function (e) {
          if (!photo) return;
          dragging = true;
          last = e;
          canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
          canvas.classList.add("dragging");
        });
        canvas.addEventListener("pointermove", function (e) {
          if (!dragging) return;
          var ratio = 1200 / canvas.clientWidth;
          offset.x += (e.clientX - last.clientX) * ratio;
          offset.y += (e.clientY - last.clientY) * ratio;
          last = e;
          render();
        });
        ["pointerup", "pointercancel"].forEach(function (type) {
          canvas.addEventListener(type, function () {
            dragging = false;
            canvas.classList.remove("dragging");
          });
        });
        canvas.addEventListener(
          "wheel",
          function (e) {
            if (!photo) return;
            e.preventDefault();
            var next = parseFloat(zoomInput.value) - e.deltaY * 0.0015;
            zoomInput.value = Math.min(3, Math.max(1, next));
            render();
          },
          { passive: false }
        );

        // Drag and drop
        ["dragenter", "dragover"].forEach(function (type) {
          preview.addEventListener(type, function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            preview.classList.add("is-over");
          });
        });
        ["dragleave", "drop"].forEach(function (type) {
          preview.addEventListener(type, function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            preview.classList.remove("is-over");
          });
        });
        preview.addEventListener("drop", function (e) {
          loadFile(e.dataTransfer.files[0]);
        });

        // Paste
        window.addEventListener("paste", function (e) {
          var items = (e.clipboardData && e.clipboardData.items) || [];
          for (var i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
            if (items[i].type.indexOf("image/") === 0) {
              loadFile(items[i].getAsFile());
              return;
            }
          }
        });

        /* ---------------- sample post ---------------- */

        var copyBtn = document.getElementById("copy-post");
        var copyStatus = document.getElementById("copy-status");

        function postPlainText() {
          var paras = document.querySelectorAll("#post-text p");
          return Array.prototype.map
            .call(paras, function (p) {
              var link = p.querySelector("a");
              if (link) {
                return p.textContent.replace(link.textContent, link.href).trim();
              }
              return p.textContent.trim();
            })
            .join("\n\n");
        }

        function flashCopied(ok) {
          copyStatus.textContent = ok ? "Copied" : "Press Ctrl+C to copy";
          copyStatus.style.color = ok ? "" : "var(--cl-black-60)";
          copyStatus.classList.add("is-visible");
          setTimeout(function () {
            copyStatus.classList.remove("is-visible");
          }, 2200);
        }

        // Fallback for browsers without clipboard access: select the text
        // so the reader only has to hit copy themselves.
        function selectPostText() {
          var range = document.createRange();
          range.selectNodeContents(document.getElementById("post-text"));
          var sel = window.getSelection();
          sel.removeAllRanges();
          sel.addRange(range);
        }

        copyBtn.addEventListener("click", function () {
          var text = postPlainText();
          if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
            navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(
              function () {
                flashCopied(true);
              },
              function () {
                selectPostText();
                flashCopied(false);
              }
            );
          } else {
            selectPostText();
            flashCopied(false);
          }
        });

        downloadBtn.addEventListener("click", function () {
          render();
          var slug =
            nameInput.value
              .trim()
              .toLowerCase()
              .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
              .replace(/^-|-$/g, "") || "attendee";
          var a = document.createElement("a");
          a.download = CONFIG.fileName + "-" + slug + ".png";
          a.href = canvas.toDataURL("image/png");
          a.click();
          shareBox.style.display = "block";
        });

        /* ---------------- setup panel (internal, ?setup=1) ---------------- */

        if (/[?&]setup=1/.test(window.location.search)) {
          var panel = document.getElementById("setup");
          panel.style.display = "block";

          var fields = [
            ["s-cx", CONFIG.photo, "cx"],
            ["s-cy", CONFIG.photo, "cy"],
            ["s-size", CONFIG.photo, "size"],
            ["s-ring", CONFIG.photo, "ringWidth"],
            ["s-tx", CONFIG, "textX"],
            ["s-mw", CONFIG, "maxWidth"],
            ["s-ny", CONFIG.name, "y"],
            ["s-ns", CONFIG.name, "size"],
            ["s-ty", CONFIG.title, "y"],
            ["s-ts", CONFIG.title, "size"],
            ["s-coy", CONFIG.company, "y"],
            ["s-cos", CONFIG.company, "size"]
          ];

          function writeConfig() {
            document.getElementById("s-out").value = JSON.stringify(
              {
                photo: CONFIG.photo,
                textX: CONFIG.textX,
                maxWidth: CONFIG.maxWidth,
                name: CONFIG.name,
                title: CONFIG.title,
                company: CONFIG.company
              },
              null,
              2
            );
          }

          fields.forEach(function (f) {
            var el = document.getElementById(f[0]);
            el.value = f[1][f[2]];
            el.addEventListener("input", function () {
              var v = parseFloat(el.value);
              if (!isNaN(v)) {
                f[1][f[2]] = v;
                render();
                writeConfig();
              }
            });
          });

          writeConfig();

          document.getElementById("s-copy").addEventListener("click", function () {
            var out = document.getElementById("s-out");
            out.select();
            if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
              navigator.clipboard.writeText(out.value);
            }
          });
        }
      })();
    </script>

Paste target: the page’s custom HTML in HubSpot. The three CSS blocks added on top of the original sit at the end of the inline <style>; the headline’s <br> is removed in the markup and also neutralised in CSS.
